\"rgba(0,0,0,0)\");\n    vignette.addColorStop(0.7, \"rgba(0,0,0,.14)\");\n    vignette.addColorStop(1, \"rgba(0,0,0,.5)\");\n    ctx.fillStyle = vignette;\n    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);\n    const topScrim = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h * 0.17);\n    topScrim.addColorStop(0, \"rgba(2, 8, 6, .34)\");\n    topScrim.addColorStop(1, \"rgba(2, 8, 6, 0)\");\n    ctx.fillStyle = topScrim;\n    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h * 0.17);\n    const bottomScrim = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, h * 0.82, 0, h);\n    bottomScrim.addColorStop(0, \"rgba(2, 8, 6, 0)\");\n    bottomScrim.addColorStop(1, \"rgba(2, 8, 6, .42)\");\n    ctx.fillStyle = bottomScrim;\n    ctx.fillRect(0, h * 0.82, w, h * 0.18);\n    this.background = c;\n  }\n\n  _buildPlantLayer() {\n    const c = document.createElement(\"canvas\");\n    c.width = Math.round(this.w * this.dpr);\n    c.height = Math.round(this.h * this.dpr);\n    const ctx = c.getContext(\"2d\");\n    ctx.setTransform(this.dpr, 0, 0, this.dpr, 0, 0);\n    const sim = this.sim;\n    if (!sim) return c;\n    const sx = this.w / GRID_W;\n    const sy = this.h / GRID_H;\n    for (let y = 0; y < GRID_H; y++) {\n      for (let x = 0; x < GRID_W; x++) {\n        const food = sim.food[y * GRID_W + x];\n        if (food < 0.028) continue;\n        const hash = ((x * 374761393) ^ (y * 668265263) ^ (x * 69069)) >>> 0;\n        const variant = hash % this.plantSprites.length;\n        const size = (7.2 + food * 10.4 + (hash % 5) * 0.35) * Math.min(sx, sy) / 9.2;\n        const px = (x + 0.5) * sx + ((hash >> 7) % 9 - 4) * 0.35;\n        const py = (y + 0.5) * sy + ((hash >> 13) % 9 - 4) * 0.35;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.14 + Math.sqrt(Math.min(1, food)) * 0.82;\n        ctx.drawImage(this.plantSprites[variant], px - size / 2, py - size / 2, size, size);\n      }\n    }\n    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n    this.plantLayer = c;\n    this.plantLayerGen = sim.gen;\n  }\n\n  _screenPos(p, alpha) {\n    const t = clamp(alpha, 0, 1);\n    const x = p.px == null ? p.x : p.px + (p.x - p.px) * t;\n    const y = p.py == null ? p.y : p.py + (p.y - p.py) * t;\n    return {\n      x: (x / WORLD_W) * this.w,\n      y: (y / WORLD_H) * this.h\n    };\n  }\n\n  _screenAngle(heading) {\n    const dx = Math.cos(heading) * (this.w / WORLD_W);\n    const dy = Math.sin(heading) * (this.h / WORLD_H);\n    return Math.atan2(dy, dx);\n  }\n\n  render(now, alpha) {\n    if (!this.sim) return;\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    ctx.setTransform(this.dpr, 0, 0, this.dpr, 0, 0);\n    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, this.w, this.h);\n    if (this.background) ctx.drawImage(this.background, 0, 0, this.w, this.h);\n    if (this.plantLayerGen !== this.sim.gen || !this.plantLayer) this._buildPlantLayer();\n    if (this.plantLayer) ctx.drawImage(this.plantLayer, 0, 0, this.w, this.h);\n    this._drawDust(now);\n    this._drawCreatures(now, alpha);\n    this._drawKillFx(now);\n    this._drawPulses(now);\n    this._drawSweep(now);\n  }\n\n  _drawDust(now) {\n    if (!this.dust) return;\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = \"lighter\";\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(211, 226, 196, .42)\";\n    for (const mote of this.dust) {\n      const drift = now * 0.0000055 * mote.drift;\n      const x = ((mote.x + Math.sin(drift + mote.phase) * mote.amplitude) % 1 + 1) % 1 * this.w;\n      const y = ((mote.y + Math.cos(drift * 0.71 + mote.phase) * mote.amplitude * 0.7) % 1 + 1) % 1 * this.h;\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.09 + 0.1 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(now * 0.0007 + mote.phase));\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(x, y, mote.size, 0, TAU);\n      ctx.fill();\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  _drawCreatures(now, alpha) {\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    const prey = this.sim.prey;\n    const predators = this.sim.predator;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = \"lighter\";\n    for (const p of prey) {\n      const pos = this._screenPos(p, alpha);\n      const pulse = 0.72 + 0.28 * Math.sin(now * 0.0023 + p.id * 1.7);\n      const energy = clamp(p.energy / 90, 0.2, 1);\n      const size = 8 + p.genes.vision * 0.38;\n      ctx.globalAlpha = (0.18 + energy * 0.33) * pulse;\n      ctx.drawImage(this.preyGlow, pos.x - size / 2, pos.y - size / 2, size, size);\n    }\n    for (const p of predators) {\n      const pos = this._screenPos(p, alpha);\n      const pulse = 0.7 + 0.3 * Math.sin(now * 0.0017 + p.id * 1.3);\n      const energy = clamp(p.energy / 150, 0.25, 1);\n      const size = 15 + p.genes.vision * 0.54;\n      ctx.globalAlpha = (0.2 + energy * 0.34) * pulse;\n      ctx.drawImage(this.predatorGlow, pos.x - size / 2, pos.y - size / 2, size, size);\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n\n    for (const p of prey) this._drawPrey(p, alpha, now);\n    for (const p of predators) this._drawPredator(p, alpha, now);\n  }\n\n  _drawPrey(p, alpha, now) {\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    const pos = this._screenPos(p, alpha);\n    const angle = this._screenAngle(p.heading);\n    const energy = clamp(p.energy / Math.max(24, p.genes.repro), 0, 1);\n    const tailLength = 4.4 + p.genes.speed * 4.2 + energy * 2.3;\n    const dx = Math.cos(angle);\n    const dy = Math.sin(angle);\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(pos.x, pos.y);\n    ctx.rotate(angle);\n    ctx.lineCap = \"round\";\n    ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(241, 185, 108, .34)\";\n    ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(-2.4, 0);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-tailLength * 0.55, Math.sin(now * 0.001 + p.id) * 1.2, -tailLength, 0);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(247, 211, 146, .23)\";\n    ctx.lineWidth = 0.55;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(-2, 0.8);\n    ctx.lineTo(-tailLength * 0.55, 2.2);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(242, 185, 104, .9)\";\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 2.65 + energy * 0.42, 1.42 + energy * 0.18, 0, 0, TAU);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(255, 236, 194, .85)\";\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.ellipse(0.5, -0.38, 0.75, 0.36, -0.2, 0, TAU);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  _drawPredator(p, alpha, now) {\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    const pos = this._screenPos(p, alpha);\n    const angle = this._screenAngle(p.heading);\n    const energy = clamp(p.energy / 140, 0, 1);\n    const scale = 0.9 + energy * 0.34;\n    const dx = Math.cos(angle);\n    const dy = Math.sin(angle);\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(pos.x, pos.y);\n    ctx.rotate(angle);\n    ctx.lineCap = \"round\";\n    ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(202, 160, 242, .24)\";\n    ctx.lineWidth = 0.66;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(-2, 0);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-8, Math.sin(now * 0.0013 + p.id) * 2.3, -13 - p.genes.speed * 1.4, 0);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(6.1 * scale, 0);\n    ctx.lineTo(-2.1 * scale, 3.4 * scale);\n    ctx.lineTo(-0.6 * scale, 0);\n    ctx.lineTo(-2.1 * scale, -3.4 * scale);\n    ctx.closePath();\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(205, 162, 243, .92)\";\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(242, 226, 255, .76)\";\n    ctx.lineWidth = 0.55;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(4.9 * scale, 0);\n    ctx.lineTo(-0.8 * scale, 0);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(245, 236, 255, .92)\";\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(0.6, 0, 0.55, 0, TAU);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  addKill(x, y, now) {\n    this.killFx.push({ x, y, start: now });\n    if (this.killFx.length > 80) this.killFx.shift();\n  }\n\n  signalEvent(event) {\n    this.pulses.push({ color: event.color, type: event.type, start: performance.now() });\n    if (this.pulses.length > 5) this.pulses.shift();\n  }\n\n  _drawKillFx(now) {\n    if (!this.killFx.length) return;\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    ctx.save();\n    for (let i = this.killFx.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n      const fx = this.killFx[i];\n      const age = (now - fx.start) / 700;\n      if (age >= 1) {\n        this.killFx.splice(i, 1);\n        continue;\n      }\n      const x = (fx.x / WORLD_W) * this.w;\n      const y = (fx.y / WORLD_H) * this.h;\n      const radius = 1.5 + age * 8;\n      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - age) * 0.42;\n      ctx.strokeStyle = \"#f1b96c\";\n      ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(x, y, radius, 0, TAU);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - age) * 0.55;\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#f7d59d\";\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(x, y, 1.1, 0, TAU);\n      ctx.fill();\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  _drawPulses(now) {\n    if (!this.pulses.length) return;\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = \"lighter\";\n    for (let i = this.pulses.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n      const pulse = this.pulses[i];\n      const age = (now - pulse.start) / 2800;\n      if (age >= 1) {\n        this.pulses.splice(i, 1);\n        continue;\n      }\n      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - age, 3);\n      const radius = eased * Math.max(this.w, this.h) * 0.68;\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.pow(1 - age, 1.7) * 0.34;\n      ctx.strokeStyle = pulse.color;\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.ellipse(this.w * 0.5, this.h * 0.51, radius, radius * 0.55, 0, 0, TAU);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      if (pulse.type === \"bloom\") {\n        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.pow(1 - age, 2.2) * 0.24;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.ellipse(this.w * 0.5, this.h * 0.51, radius * 0.72, radius * 0.4, 0, 0, TAU);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  _drawSweep(now) {\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    const period = 26000;\n    const phase = (now % period) / period;\n    const x = phase * this.w;\n    const halo = ctx.createLinearGradient(x - 110, 0, x + 26, 0);\n    halo.addColorStop(0, \"rgba(170, 207, 158, 0)\");\n    halo.addColorStop(0.76, \"rgba(170, 207, 158, .018)\");\n    halo.addColorStop(1, \"rgba(201, 232, 191, .035)\");\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = \"screen\";\n    ctx.fillStyle = halo;\n    ctx.fillRect(x - 110, 0, 136, this.h);\n    ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(212, 234, 199, .06)\";\n    ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(x, 0);\n    ctx.lineTo(x, this.h);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}\n\nclass InstrumentDial {\n  constructor(element, options) {\n    this.el = element;\n    this.min = options.min;\n    this.max = options.max;\n    this.step = options.step;\n    this.defaultValue = options.value;\n    this.value = options.value;\n    this.ariaFormat = options.ariaFormat;\n    this.onInput = options.onInput;\n    this.dragging = false;\n    this.render();\n    this.bind();\n  }\n\n  quantize(value) {\n    const stepped = this.min + Math.round((value - this.min) / this.step) * this.step;\n    const decimals = String(this.step).split(\".\")[1];\n    const precision = decimals ? decimals.length : 0;\n    return clamp(Number(stepped.toFixed(precision)), this.min, this.max);\n  }\n\n  set(value, emit = false) {\n    const next = this.quantize(value);\n    const changed = next !== this.value;\n    this.value = next;\n    this.render();\n    if (changed && emit && this.onInput) this.onInput(next);\n  }\n\n  render() {\n    const fraction = (this.value - this.min) / (this.max - this.min);\n    const angle = -135 + fraction * 270;\n    this.el.style.setProperty(\"--angle\", angle.toFixed(2) + \"deg\");\n    this.el.style.setProperty(\"--sweep\", (fraction * 270).toFixed(2));\n    this.el.setAttribute(\"aria-valuenow\", String(this.value));\n    this.el.setAttribute(\"aria-valuetext\", this.ariaFormat(this.value));\n  }\n\n  valueFromPointer(event) {\n    const rect = this.el.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const cx = rect.left + rect.width / 2;\n    const cy = rect.top + rect.height / 2;\n    const raw = Math.atan2(event.clientX - cx, cy - event.clientY) * 180 / Math.PI;\n    const angle = clamp(raw, -135, 135);\n    const fraction = (angle + 135) / 270;\n    return this.min + fraction * (this.max - this.min);\n  }\n\n  bind() {\n    this.el.addEventListener(\"pointerdown\", (event) => {\n      if (event.button !== 0) return;\n      event.preventDefault();\n      this.dragging = true;\n      this.el.focus({ preventScroll: true });\n      this.el.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);\n      this.set(this.valueFromPointer(event), true);\n    });\n    this.el.addEventListener(\"pointermove\", (event) => {\n      if (!this.dragging) return;\n      this.set(this.valueFromPointer(event), true);\n    });\n    const stop = (event) => {\n      if (!this.dragging) return;\n      this.dragging = false;\n      if (this.el.hasPointerCapture(event.pointerId)) this.el.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);\n    };\n    this.el.addEventListener(\"pointerup\", stop);\n    this.el.addEventListener(\"pointercancel\", stop);\n    this.el.addEventListener(\"keydown\", (event) => {\n      let next = this.value;\n      if (event.key === \"ArrowRight\" || event.key === \"ArrowUp\") next += this.step;\n      else if (event.key === \"ArrowLeft\" || event.key === \"ArrowDown\") next -= this.step;\n      else if (event.key === \"PageUp\") next += this.step * 10;\n      else if (event.key === \"PageDown\") next -= this.step * 10;\n      else if (event.key === \"Home\") next = this.min;\n      else if (event.key === \"End\") next = this.max;\n      else return;\n      event.preventDefault();\n      this.set(next, true);\n    });\n    this.el.addEventListener(\"dblclick\", (event) => {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      this.set(this.defaultValue, true);\n    });\n  }\n}\n\nclass TooltipLayer {\n  constructor(element) {\n    this.el = element;\n    this.current = null;\n    this.timer = null;\n    this.bindAll();\n    window.addEventListener(\"scroll\", () => this.hide(), true);\n    window.addEventListener(\"resize\", () => this.hide());\n  }\n\n  bindAll() {\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-tip]\").forEach((anchor) => {\n      anchor.addEventListener(\"pointerenter\", () => this.show(anchor));\n      anchor.addEventListener(\"pointerleave\", () => this.hide());\n      anchor.addEventListener(\"focusin\", () => this.show(anchor));\n      anchor.addEventListener(\"focusout\", (event) => {\n        if (!anchor.contains(event.relatedTarget)) this.hide();\n      });\n    });\n  }\n\n  show(anchor) {\n    if (!anchor.dataset.tip) return;\n    clearTimeout(this.timer);\n    const label = anchor.dataset.tipTitle\n      || (anchor.querySelector(\"label, .control-label\") || {}).textContent\n      || anchor.getAttribute(\"aria-label\")\n      || \"Instrument note\";\n    const keys = anchor.dataset.keys;\n    this.el.innerHTML = \"<b>\" + this.escape(label.trim()) + \"</b>\" + this.escape(anchor.dataset.tip)\n      + (keys ? \"<br><kbd>\" + this.escape(keys) + \"</kbd>\" : \"\");\n    this.el.classList.add(\"is-visible\");\n    this.el.setAttribute(\"aria-hidden\", \"false\");\n    const rect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const tipRect = this.el.getBoundingClientRect();\n    let left = rect.left + rect.width / 2 - tipRect.width / 2;\n    left = clamp(left, 10, window.innerWidth - tipRect.width - 10);\n    let top = rect.top - tipRect.height - 9;\n    if (top < 8) top = rect.bottom + 9;\n    top = clamp(top, 8, window.innerHeight - tipRect.height - 8);\n    this.el.style.left = left + \"px\";\n    this.el.style.top = top + \"px\";\n    this.current = anchor;\n  }\n\n  hide() {\n    clearTimeout(this.timer);\n    this.el.classList.remove(\"is-visible\");\n    this.el.setAttribute(\"aria-hidden\", \"true\");\n    this.current = null;\n  }\n\n  escape(text) {\n    return String(text).replace(/[&<>\"']/g, (ch) => ({ \"&\": \"&amp;\", \"<\": \"&lt;\", \">\": \"&gt;\", '\"': \"&quot;\", \"'\": \"&#39;\" }[ch]));\n  }\n}\n\nclass ReportRenderer {\n  constructor(app) {\n    this.app = app;\n    this.census = this.drawTraits();\n  }\n\n  plot(event) {}\n\n  drawCensus() {\n    const sim = this.app.sim;\n    const { ctx, width, height } = fitCanvas(this.census);\n    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n    if (width < 40 || height < 40) return;\n    const history = sim.history;\n    const final = history[history.length - 1];\n    const maxGen = Math.max(1, final.gen);\n    const pad = { left: 76, right: 15, top: 25, bottom: 27 };\n    const plotW = Math.max(10, width - pad.left - pad.right);\n    const plotH = Math.max(10, height - pad.top - pad.bottom);\n    const events = sim.events;\n    const species = [\n      { key: \"flora\", label: \"Flora\", color: \"#667f33\", glyph: \"flora\", unit: \"colonies\" },\n      { key: \"prey\", label: \"Grazers\", color: \"#ac6720\", glyph: \"prey\", unit: \"individuals\" },\n      { key: \"predator\", label: \"Hunters\", color: \"#70518f\", glyph: \"predator\", unit: \"individuals\" }\n    ];\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.font = '500 6px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n    ctx.textBaseline = \"middle\";\n    const eventColor = (type) => ({ drought: \"#b66536\", plague: \"#a9506d\", bloom: \"#37877f\" }[type] || \"#9b7a40\");\n    for (let i = 0; i < events.length; i++) {\n      const event = events[i];\n      const x = pad.left + (event.gen / maxGen) * plotW;\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.22;\n      ctx.strokeStyle = eventColor(event.type);\n      ctx.setLineDash([2, 4]);\n      ctx.lineWidth = 0.7;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(x, pad.top - 9);\n      ctx.lineTo(x, pad.top + plotH);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.setLineDash([]);\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n      ctx.fillStyle = eventColor(event.type);\n      const symbolY = pad.top - 14;\n      this.drawEventMark(ctx, event.type, x, symbolY, 3.1, eventColor(event.type));\n      ctx.font = '500 5.5px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .5)\";\n      ctx.textAlign = \"center\";\n      ctx.fillText(event.roman, x, symbolY - 9);\n    }\n    const laneGap = 8;\n    const laneH = (plotH - laneGap * 2) / 3;\n    species.forEach((sp, laneIndex) => {\n      const laneTop = pad.top + laneIndex * (laneH + laneGap);\n      const values = history.map((h) => h.counts[sp.key]);\n      const peak = Math.max(1, ...values);\n      const bottom = laneTop + laneH;\n      ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .11)\";\n      ctx.lineWidth = 0.55;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(pad.left, bottom + 0.5);\n      ctx.lineTo(pad.left + plotW, bottom + 0.5);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .025)\";\n      ctx.fillRect(pad.left, laneTop, plotW, laneH);\n      ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .05)\";\n      ctx.setLineDash([1, 5]);\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(pad.left, laneTop + laneH * 0.34);\n      ctx.lineTo(pad.left + plotW, laneTop + laneH * 0.34);\n      ctx.moveTo(pad.left, laneTop + laneH * 0.67);\n      ctx.lineTo(pad.left + plotW, laneTop + laneH * 0.67);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.setLineDash([]);\n\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.rect(pad.left, laneTop - 1, plotW, laneH + 2);\n      ctx.clip();\n      const xAt = (gen) => pad.left + (gen / maxGen) * plotW;\n      const yAt = (value) => bottom - (value / peak) * (laneH - 5);\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(xAt(history[0].gen), yAt(history[0].counts[sp.key]));\n      for (let i = 1; i < history.length; i++) {\n        const h = history[i];\n        ctx.lineTo(xAt(h.gen), hexToRgba(sp.color, 0.015));\n      ctx.lineTo(xAt(final.gen), bottom);\n      ctx.lineTo(xAt(history[0].gen), bottom);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.fillStyle = grad;\n      ctx.fill();\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(xAt(history[0].gen), yAt(history[0].counts[sp.key]));\n      for (let i = 1; i < history.length; i++) {\n        const h = history[i];\n        ctx.lineTo(xAt(h.gen), yAt(h.counts[sp.key]));\n      }\n      ctx.strokeStyle = sp.color;\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1.3;\n      ctx.lineJoin = \"round\";\n      ctx.stroke();\n      const finalValue = final.counts[sp.key];\n      const dotX = xAt(final.gen);\n      const dotY = yAt(finalValue);\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#f1ebdc\";\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(dotX, dotY, 2.3, 0, TAU);\n      ctx.fill();\n      ctx.strokeStyle = sp.color;\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(dotX, dotY, 2.3, 0, TAU);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.restore();\n\n      ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n      ctx.font = '500 6.5px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .48)\";\n      ctx.fillText(sp.label.toUpperCase(), pad.left - 11, laneTop + 9);\n      ctx.font = '400 13px \"Charter\", Georgia, serif';\n      ctx.fillStyle = sp.color;\n      ctx.fillText(formatInteger(finalValue), pad.left - 11, laneTop + 24);\n      ctx.font = '400 5.5px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .34)\";\n      ctx.fillText(sp.unit, pad.left - 11, laneTop + 34);\n      ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n      ctx.font = '400 5.5px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .32)\";\n      ctx.fillText(\"peak \" + formatInteger(peak), pad.left + plotW - 2, laneTop + 7);\n    });\n\n    ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .25)\";\n    ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(pad.left, pad.top + plotH + 0.5);\n    ctx.lineTo(pad.left + plotW, pad.top + plotH + 0.5);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .47)\";\n    ctx.font = '400 5.5px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n    ctx.textAlign = \"center\";\n    ctx.textBaseline = \"top\";\n    for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {\n      const gen = Math.round((maxGen * i) / 4);\n      const x = pad.left + (gen / maxGen) * plotW;\n      ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .25)\";\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(x, pad.top + plotH + 2);\n      ctx.lineTo(x, pad.top + plotH + 5);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.fillText(String(gen).padStart(3, \"0\"), x, pad.top + plotH + 8);\n    }\n    ctx.textAlign = \"left\";\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .38)\";\n    ctx.fillText(\"GENERATION\", pad.left, height - 10);\n    ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n    ctx.fillText(maxGen + \" GENERATIONS OBSERVED\", pad.left + plotW, height - 10);\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  drawEventMark(ctx, type, x, y, size, color) {\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.strokeStyle = color;\n    ctx.fillStyle = color;\n    ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;\n    if (type === \"drought\") {\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(x, y, size * 0.56, 0, TAU);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {\n        const angle = (i / 6) * TAU;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(x + Math.cos(angle) * size * 0.9, y + Math.sin(angle) * size * 0.9);\n        ctx.lineTo(x + Math.cos(angle) * size * 1.25, y + Math.sin(angle) * size * 1.25);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n    } else if (type === \"plague\") {\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(x, y, size * 0.62, 0, TAU);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {\n        const angle = (i / 4) * TAU + 0.4;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(x + Math.cos(angle) * size * 1.15, y + Math.sin(angle) * size * 1.15, size * 0.25, 0, TAU);\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n    } else {\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n        const angle = (i / 3) * TAU - 1.2;\n        ctx.ellipse(x + Math.cos(angle) * size * 0.6, y + Math.sin(angle) * size * 0.6, size * 0.66, size * 0.38, angle, 0, TAU);\n      }\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(x, y, size * 0.22, 0, TAU);\n      ctx.fill();\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  drawTraits() {\n    const sim = this.app.sim;\n    const { ctx, width, height } = fitCanvas(this.traits);\n    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n    if (width < 40 || height < 40) return;\n    const history = sim.history;\n    const finalGen = Math.max(1, history[history.length - 1].gen);\n    const gap = Math.max(12, width * 0.016);\n    const panelW = (width - gap * 3) / 4;\n    const panelTop = 24;\n    const plotHeight = height - 58;\n    const colors = { prey: \"#ac6720\", predator: \"#70518f\" };\n    GENE_DEFS.forEach((gene, index) => {\n      const x0 = index * (panelW + gap);\n      const plotX = x0 + 24;\n      const plotY = panelTop;\n      const plotW = panelW - 30;\n      let low = Infinity;\n      let high = -Infinity;\n      for (const h of history) {\n        for (const kind of [\"prey\", \"predator\"]) {\n          const entry = h.genes[kind];\n          if (!entry) continue;\n          const stat = entry[gene.key];\n          low = Math.min(low, stat.mean - stat.sd);\n          high = Math.max(high, stat.mean + stat.sd);\n        }\n      }\n      if (!Number.isFinite(low) || !Number.isFinite(high)) {\n        low = 0;\n        high = 1;\n      }\n      let span = high - low;\n      if (span < 0.0001) span = Math.max(0.2, Math.abs(high) * 0.1 || 1);\n      const pad = span * 0.14;\n      low -= pad;\n      high += pad;\n      const yAt = (value) => plotY + plotHeight - ((value - low) / (high - low)) * plotHeight;\n      const xAt = (gen) => plotX + (gen / finalGen) * plotW;\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .23)\";\n      ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(plotX, plotY);\n      ctx.lineTo(plotX, plotY + plotHeight);\n      ctx.lineTo(plotX + plotW, plotY + plotHeight);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      const gridCount = 3;\n      for (let g = 0; g < gridCount; g++) {\n        const val = low + (high - low) * (g / (gridCount - 1));\n        const y = yAt(val);\n        ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .08)\";\n        ctx.setLineDash([1, 4]);\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(plotX, y);\n        ctx.lineTo(plotX + plotW, y);\n        ctx.stroke();\n        ctx.setLineDash([]);\n        ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .44)\";\n        ctx.font = '400 5.2px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n        ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n        ctx.textBaseline = \"middle\";\n        ctx.fillText(gene.decimals === 0 ? Math.round(val).toString() : val.toFixed(gene.decimals), plotX - 4, y);\n      }\n      const eventColor = (type) => ({ drought: \"#b66536\", plague: \"#a9506d\", bloom: \"#37877f\" }[type] || \"#9b7a40\");\n      for (const event of sim.events) {\n        const x = xAt(event.gen);\n        ctx.strokeStyle = hexToRgba(eventColor(event.type), 0.14);\n        ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;\n        ctx.setLineDash([2, 4]);\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(x, plotY);\n        ctx.lineTo(x, plotY + plotHeight);\n        ctx.stroke();\n        ctx.setLineDash([]);\n        ctx.fillStyle = eventColor(event.type);\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(x, plotY - 4, 1.15, 0, TAU);\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.rect(plotX, plotY - 1, plotW, plotHeight + 2);\n      ctx.clip();\n      for (const kind of [\"prey\", \"predator\"]) {\n        const points = [];\n        for (const h of history) {\n          const entry = h.genes[kind];\n          if (!entry) continue;\n          const stat = entry[gene.key];\n          points.push({ gen: h.gen, mean: stat.mean, sd: stat.sd });\n        }\n        if (!points.length) continue;\n        const upper = [];\n        const lower = [];\n        for (const p of points) {\n          upper.push([xAt(p.gen), yAt(p.mean + p.sd)]);\n          lower.push([xAt(p.gen), yAt(p.mean - p.sd)]);\n        }\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(upper[0][0], upper[0][1]);\n        for (let i = 1; i < upper.length; i++) ctx.lineTo(upper[i][0], upper[i][1]);\n        for (let i = lower.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) ctx.lineTo(lower[i][0], lower[i][1]);\n        ctx.closePath();\n        ctx.fillStyle = hexToRgba(colors[kind], 0.105);\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(xAt(points[0].gen), yAt(points[0].mean));\n        for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) ctx.lineTo(xAt(points[i].gen), yAt(points[i].mean));\n        ctx.strokeStyle = colors[kind];\n        ctx.lineWidth = 1.15;\n        ctx.lineJoin = \"round\";\n        ctx.stroke();\n        const last = points[points.length - 1];\n        const lastX = xAt(last.gen);\n        const lastY = yAt(last.mean);\n        ctx.fillStyle = \"#f1ebdc\";\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(lastX, lastY, 1.8, 0, TAU);\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.strokeStyle = colors[kind];\n        ctx.lineWidth = 0.9;\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n      ctx.restore();\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .85)\";\n      ctx.font = '500 7px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n      ctx.textAlign = \"left\";\n      ctx.textBaseline = \"alphabetic\";\n      ctx.fillText(gene.label.toUpperCase(), x0, 9);\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .38)\";\n      ctx.font = '400 5.2px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n      ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n      ctx.fillText(gene.unit, x0 + panelW, 9);\n      ctx.textAlign = \"left\";\n      ctx.font = '400 5.2px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .35)\";\n      ctx.fillText(\"GEN 0\", x0, height - 20);\n      ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n      ctx.fillText(String(finalGen), x0 + panelW, height - 20);\n      const final = history[history.length - 1];\n      const finalPrey = final.genes.prey ? final.genes.prey[gene.key] : null;\n      const finalPred = final.genes.predator ? final.genes.predator[gene.key] : null;\n      ctx.textAlign = \"left\";\n      ctx.font = '400 5.5px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n      ctx.fillStyle = colors.prey;\n      if (finalPrey) ctx.fillText(\"P \" + this.formatMean(gene, finalPrey), x0, height - 10);\n      ctx.fillStyle = colors.predator;\n      if (finalPred) {\n        ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n        ctx.fillText(\"H \" + this.formatMean(gene, finalPred), x0 + panelW, height - 10);\n      }\n      if (index < 3) {\n        ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(20, 35, 29, .11)\";\n        ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(x0 + panelW + gap / 2, 2);\n        ctx.lineTo(x0 + panelW + gap / 2, height - 15);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  formatMean(gene, stat) {\n    const mean = gene.decimals === 0 ? Math.round(stat.mean).toString() : stat.mean.toFixed(gene.decimals);\n    const sd = gene.decimals === 0 ? Math.round(stat.sd).toString() : stat.sd.toFixed(gene.decimals);\n    return mean + \" ±\" + sd;\n  }\n}\n\nclass VivariumApp {\n  constructor() {\n    this.state = {\n      seed: 1072,\n      generations: 180,\n      mutation: 3.8,\n      regrowth: 0.9,\n      speed: 6,\n      catastropheEvery: 34,\n      events: { drought: true, plague: true, bloom: true }\n    };\n    this.playing = true;\n    this.completed = false;\n    this.reportOpen = false;\n    this.acc = 0;\n    this.lastTime = performance.now();\n    this.lastHud = 0;\n    this.lastMicro = 0;\n    this.lastThroughputSample = 0;\n    this.stepTimes = [];\n    this.throughput = [0];\n    this.lastNote = 0;\n    this.bannerTimer = null;\n    this.bannerHideTimer = null;\n    this.washTimer = null;\n    this.reportTimer = null;\n    this.sim = this.createSimulation();\n    this.dom = this.collectDom();\n    this.renderer = new WorldRenderer(this.dom.worldCanvas, this.sim);\n    this.renderer.setSim(this.sim);\n    this.reportRenderer = new ReportRenderer(this);\n    this.tooltips = new TooltipLayer(this.dom.tooltip);\n    this.dials = this.createDials();\n    this.bindControls();\n    this.bindGlobal();\n    this.syncControls();\n    this.renderer.resize();\n    this.updateAll(true);\n    this.drawTimeline(performance.now());\n    this.drawMicros(performance.now(), true);\n    this.frame = this.frame.bind(this);\n    requestAnimationFrame(this.frame);\n    this.updateFieldNote(true);\n  }\n\n  collectDom() {\n    const ids = [\n      \"worldWrap\", \"worldCanvas\", \"playBtn\", \"playIcon\", \"stepBtn\", \"transportState\",\n      \"timelineCanvas\", \"hudGen\", \"hudTarget\", \"genNow\", \"genTarget\", \"countFlora\",\n      \"countPrey\", \"countPredator\", \"metaSeed\", \"metaState\", \"sealProgress\", \"sealWord\",\n      \"liveTag\", \"seedInput\", \"seedDown\", \"seedUp\", \"seedShuffle\", \"genInput\", \"genDown\",\n      \"genUp\", \"regrowthInput\", \"regrowthOutput\", \"speedInput\", \"speedOutput\",\n      \"mutationOutput\", \"intervalOutput\", \"mutationDial\", \"intervalDial\", \"disturbanceCount\",\n      \"fieldNote\", \"noteSerial\", \"eventBanner\", \"eventWash\", \"eventGlyphUse\", \"eventKicker\",\n      \"eventTitle\", \"eventDetail\", \"finishBtn\", \"report\", \"reportDeck\", \"reportMeta\",\n      \"reportSeed\", \"reportGenerations\", \"outcomeLede\", \"finalCounts\", \"eventLedger\",\n      \"ledgerCount\", \"extendBtn\", \"replayBtn\", \"newBtn\", \"announcer\", \"specimen\",\n      \"apparatus\", \"tooltip\", \"censusChart\", \"traitsChart\", \"console\"\n    ];\n    const out = {};\n    for (const id of ids) out[id] = document.getElementById(id);\n    out.toggles = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(\".toggle\"));\n    out.controls = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(\".control\"));\n    out.micros = new Map();\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"canvas[data-micro]\").forEach((canvas) => {\n      out.micros.set(canvas.dataset.micro, canvas);\n    });\n    return out;\n  }\n\n  createSimulation() {\n    return new VivariumSimulation({\n      seed: this.state.seed,\n      generations: this.state.generations,\n      mutationRate: this.state.mutation / 100,\n      regrowth: this.state.regrowth,\n      catastropheEvery: this.state.catastropheEvery,\n      enabledEvents: this.enabledEventTypes()\n    });\n  }\n\n  enabledEventTypes() {\n    return Object.keys(this.state.events).filter((type) => this.state.events[type]);\n  }\n\n  createDials() {\n    const mutation = new InstrumentDial(this.dom.mutationDial, {\n      min: 0.5,\n      max: 12,\n      step: 0.1,\n      value: this.state.mutation,\n      ariaFormat: (value) => value.toFixed(1) + \" percent per gene per birth\",\n      onInput: (value) => this.setMutation(value)\n    });\n    const interval = new InstrumentDial(this.dom.intervalDial, {\n      min: 10,\n      max: 120,\n      step: 1,\n      value: this.state.catastropheEvery,\n      ariaFormat: (value) => \"Catastrophe every \" + Math.round(value) + \" generations\",\n      onInput: (value) => this.setCatastropheEvery(value)\n    });\n    return { mutation, interval };\n  }\n\n  bindControls() {\n    this.dom.playBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.togglePlayback());\n    this.dom.stepBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.stepOne());\n    this.dom.finishBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.finishRun(180, true));\n\n    this.dom.seedDown.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.setSeed(this.state.seed - 1));\n    this.dom.seedUp.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.setSeed(this.state.seed + 1));\n    this.dom.seedShuffle.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.setSeed(nextSeedFrom(this.state.seed)));\n    this.dom.seedInput.addEventListener(\"change\", () => this.applySeedInput());\n    this.dom.seedInput.addEventListener(\"keydown\", (event) => {\n      if (event.key === \"Enter\") {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        this.applySeedInput();\n        this.dom.seedInput.blur();\n      }\n    });\n\n    this.dom.genDown.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.applyGenerations(this.state.generations - 10));\n    this.dom.genUp.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.applyGenerations(this.state.generations + 10));\n    this.dom.genInput.addEventListener(\"change\", () => this.applyGenerationsInput());\n    this.dom.genInput.addEventListener(\"keydown\", (event) => {\n      if (event.key === \"Enter\") {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        this.applyGenerationsInput();\n        this.dom.genInput.blur();\n      }\n    });\n\n    this.dom.regrowthInput.addEventListener(\"input\", (event) => this.setRegrowth(Number(event.target.value)));\n    this.dom.speedInput.addEventListener(\"input\", (event) => this.setSpeed(Number(event.target.value)));\n\n    this.dom.toggles.forEach((toggle) => {\n      toggle.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.toggleEvent(toggle.dataset.event));\n    });\n\n    this.dom.replayBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", () => {\n      this.closeReport(false);\n      this.restart(this.state.seed, true);\n    });\n    this.dom.newBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", () => {\n      this.closeReport(false);\n      this.restart(nextSeedFrom(this.state.seed), true);\n    });\n    this.dom.extendBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", () => this.extendRun());\n  }\n\n  bindGlobal() {\n    document.addEventListener(\"keydown\", (event) => {\n      if (this.reportOpen) {\n        if (event.key === \"Tab\") this.trapReportFocus(event);\n        return;\n      }\n      const target = event.target;\n      const interactive = target.closest(\"button, input, select, textarea, [role='slider'], [role='switch'], a\");\n      if (!interactive && event.code === \"Space\") {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        this.togglePlayback();\n      } else if (!interactive && event.key === \"ArrowRight\") {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        this.stepOne();\n      }\n    });\n    window.addEventListener(\"resize\", () => {\n      this.renderer.resize();\n      this.drawTimeline(performance.now());\n      if (this.reportOpen) this.reportRenderer.render();\n    });\n  }\n\n  trapReportFocus(event) {\n    const focusable = Array.from(this.dom.report.querySelectorAll(\"button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex='-1'])\"))\n      .filter((el) => !el.disabled && el.offsetParent !== null);\n    if (!focusable.length) return;\n    const first = focusable[0];\n    const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];\n    if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      last.focus();\n    } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n      first.focus();\n    }\n  }\n\n  frame(now) {\n    const dt = Math.min(0.1, Math.max(0, (now - this.lastTime) / 1000));\n    this.lastTime = now;\n    if (this.playing && !this.reportOpen) {\n      this.acc += dt * this.state.speed;\n      let steps = 0;\n      while (this.acc >= 1 && steps < 14 && !this.completed) {\n        const result = this.sim.step();\n        this.consumeStep(result);\n        this.stepTimes.push(now);\n        this.acc -= 1;\n        steps++;\n        if (this.sim.gen >= this.state.generations) {\n          this.acc = 0;\n          this.finishRun(720, false);\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n      if (steps === 0) this.acc = Math.min(this.acc, 1.2);\n    }\n    if (now - this.lastThroughputSample > 650) {\n      this.lastThroughputSample = now;\n      this.stepTimes = this.stepTimes.filter((time) => now - time < 1600);\n      const measured = this.stepTimes.filter((time) => now - time < 1000).length;\n      this.throughput.push(measured);\n      if (this.throughput.length > 40) this.throughput.shift();\n    }\n    const alpha = this.playing && !this.reportOpen && this.state.speed <= 24 ? clamp(this.acc, 0, 1) : 1;\n    this.renderer.render(now, alpha);\n    this.drawTimeline(now);\n    if (now - this.lastHud > 90) {\n      this.lastHud = now;\n      this.updateLiveUI(now);\n    }\n    if (now - this.lastMicro > 240) {\n      this.lastMicro = now;\n      this.drawMicros(now);\n    }\n    requestAnimationFrame(this.frame);\n  }\n\n  consumeStep(result) {\n    const now = performance.now();\n    if (result.kills.length) {\n      for (const kill of result.kills) this.renderer.addKill(kill.x, kill.y, now);\n    }\n    for (const event of result.events) this.announceEvent(event);\n    if (result.events.length) this.updateFieldNote(true);\n  }\n\n  announceEvent(event) {\n    const meta = EVENT_META[event.type];\n    this.dom.eventBanner.hidden = false;\n    this.dom.eventBanner.style.setProperty(\"--event-color\", event.color);\n    this.dom.eventBanner.style.setProperty(\"--event-line\", hexToRgba(event.color, 0.5));\n    this.dom.eventGlyphUse.setAttribute(\"href\", meta.glyph);\n    this.dom.eventKicker.textContent = \"Disturbance \" + padNumber(event.seq, 2) + \" · Generation \" + padNumber(event.gen, 3);\n    this.dom.eventTitle.firstChild.textContent = event.label + \" \";\n    this.dom.eventDetail.textContent = event.detail;\n    this.dom.eventBanner.classList.remove(\"is-showing\", \"is-leaving\");\n    void this.dom.eventBanner.offsetWidth;\n    this.dom.eventBanner.classList.add(\"is-showing\");\n    clearTimeout(this.bannerTimer);\n    clearTimeout(this.bannerHideTimer);\n    this.bannerTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n      this.dom.eventBanner.classList.add(\"is-leaving\");\n      this.bannerHideTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n        this.dom.eventBanner.hidden = true;\n        this.dom.eventBanner.classList.remove(\"is-showing\", \"is-leaving\");\n      }, 520);\n    }, 3000);\n\n    const wash = this.dom.eventWash;\n    const alpha = event.type === \"drought\" ? 0.22 : event.type === \"plague\" ? 0.15 : 0.17;\n    const tint = hexToRgba(event.color, alpha);\n    wash.style.background = event.type === \"drought\"\n      ? \"radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 115%, \" + tint + \", transparent 68%), linear-gradient(180deg, \" + hexToRgba(event.color, alpha * 0.35) + \", transparent 44%)\"\n      : event.type === \"plague\"\n        ? \"radial-gradient(circle at 51% 46%, \" + tint + \", transparent 65%), linear-gradient(180deg, transparent, \" + hexToRgba(event.color, alpha * 0.3) + \")\"\n        : \"radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 102%, \" + tint + \", transparent 68%), radial-gradient(circle at 50% 42%, \" + hexToRgba(event.color, alpha * 0.55) + \", transparent 60%)\";\n    wash.classList.remove(\"is-active\");\n    void wash.offsetWidth;\n    wash.classList.add(\"is-active\");\n    clearTimeout(this.washTimer);\n    this.washTimer = setTimeout(() => wash.classList.remove(\"is-active\"), 1450);\n\n    this.renderer.signalEvent(event);\n    this.dom.announcer.textContent = event.label + \" at generation \" + event.gen + \". \" + event.detail + \".\";\n  }\n\n  setMutation(value) {\n    this.state.mutation = clamp(Number(value), 0.5, 12);\n    this.sim.setMutationRate(this.state.mutation / 100);\n    this.updateControlReadouts();\n    this.drawMicros(performance.now(), true);\n  }\n\n  setRegrowth(value) {\n    this.state.regrowth = clamp(Number(value), 0.2, 2);\n    this.sim.setRegrowth(this.state.regrowth);\n    this.updateControlReadouts();\n    this.drawMicros(performance.now(), true);\n  }\n\n  setSpeed(value) {\n    this.state.speed = clamp(Math.round(Number(value)), 1, 60);\n    this.updateControlReadouts();\n    this.drawMicros(performance.now(), true);\n  }\n\n  setCatastropheEvery(value) {\n    this.state.catastropheEvery = clamp(Math.round(Number(value)), 10, 120);\n    this.sim.setCatastropheInterval(this.state.catastropheEvery);\n    this.updateControlReadouts();\n    this.drawTimeline(performance.now());\n    this.drawMicros(performance.now(), true);\n  }\n\n  setSeed(seed) {\n    const normalized = normalizeSeed(seed);\n    if (normalized === this.state.seed) return;\n    this.restart(normalized, false);\n  }\n\n  applySeedInput() {\n    const value = Number(this.dom.seedInput.value);\n    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {\n      this.dom.seedInput.value = String(this.state.seed);\n      return;\n    }\n    const normalized = normalizeSeed(value);\n    this.dom.seedInput.value = String(normalized);\n    if (normalized !== this.state.seed) this.restart(normalized, false);\n  }\n\n  applyGenerations(value) {\n    const parsed = Number(value);\n    if (!Number.isFinite(parsed)) {\n      this.dom.genInput.value = String(this.state.generations);\n      return;\n    }\n    const next = clamp(Math.round(parsed), 40, 1000);\n    this.state.generations = next;\n    this.dom.genInput.value = String(next);\n    this.sim.target = next;\n    this.updateControlReadouts();\n    this.updateLiveUI(performance.now());\n    if (this.sim.gen >= next) {\n      this.finishRun(320, true);\n    } else if (this.completed) {\n      this.completed = false;\n      this.setPlaying(true);\n    }\n  }\n\n  applyGenerationsInput() {\n    this.applyGenerations(this.dom.genInput.value);\n    this.dom.genInput.value = String(this.state.generations);\n  }\n\n  toggleEvent(type) {\n    this.state.events[type] = !this.state.events[type];\n    this.sim.setEnabledEvents(this.enabledEventTypes());\n    this.updateToggles();\n    this.updateControlReadouts();\n    this.drawMicros(performance.now(), true);\n  }\n\n  restart(seed, focusPlay) {\n    clearTimeout(this.reportTimer);\n    this.reportTimer = null;\n    this.closeReport(false);\n    this.state.seed = normalizeSeed(seed);\n    this.completed = false;\n    this.playing = true;\n    this.acc = 0;\n    this.throughput = [0];\n    this.stepTimes = [];\n    this.lastThroughputSample = performance.now();\n    this.dom.seedInput.value = String(this.state.seed);\n    this.dom.genInput.value = String(this.state.generations);\n    this.sim = this.createSimulation();\n    this.renderer.setSim(this.sim);\n    this.hideEventVisuals();\n    this.lastTime = performance.now();\n    this.lastHud = 0;\n    this.lastMicro = 0;\n    this.lastNote = 0;\n    this.updateAll(true);\n    this.updateFieldNote(true);\n    this.dom.announcer.textContent = \"New chamber seeded with \" + this.state.seed + \". Observation resumed.\";\n    if (focusPlay) this.dom.playBtn.focus({ preventScroll: true });\n    this.drawTimeline(performance.now());\n    this.drawMicros(performance.now(), true);\n  }\n\n  extendRun() {\n    if (!this.reportOpen) return;\n    const next = Math.max(this.state.generations + 60, this.sim.gen + 60);\n    this.state.generations = Math.min(1000, next);\n    this.sim.target = this.state.generations;\n    this.completed = false;\n    this.acc = 0;\n    this.lastTime = performance.now();\n    this.closeReport(true);\n    this.setPlaying(true);\n    this.dom.genInput.value = String(this.state.generations);\n    this.updateAll(true);\n    this.dom.announcer.textContent = \"Observation extended to generation \" + this.state.generations + \".\";\n  }\n\n  togglePlayback() {\n    if (this.reportOpen) return;\n    if (this.sim.gen >= this.state.generations) return;\n    this.setPlaying(!this.playing);\n  }\n\n  setPlaying(playing) {\n    if (this.completed && playing) return;\n    this.playing = playing;\n    if (!playing) this.acc = 0;\n    this.lastTime = performance.now();\n    this.updatePlaybackUI();\n  }\n\n  stepOne() {\n    if (this.reportOpen) return;\n    if (this.sim.gen >= this.state.generations) return;\n    if (this.playing) this.setPlaying(false);\n    const result = this.sim.step();\n    this.consumeStep(result);\n    this.acc = 0;\n    this.updateAll(true);\n    this.drawTimeline(performance.now());\n    this.drawMicros(performance.now(), true);\n    if (this.sim.gen >= this.state.generations) this.finishRun(420, false);\n  }\n\n  finishRun(delay, truncate) {\n    if (this.completed || this.reportOpen) return;\n    clearTimeout(this.reportTimer);\n    this.playing = false;\n    this.acc = 0;\n    if (truncate && this.sim.gen > 0) {\n      this.sim.target = this.sim.gen;\n      this.state.generations = this.sim.gen;\n      this.dom.genInput.value = String(this.state.generations);\n      this.dom.genTarget.textContent = String(this.state.generations);\n    }\n    this.completed = true;\n    this.updatePlaybackUI();\n    this.updateLiveUI(performance.now());\n    this.dom.announcer.textContent = \"Observation complete. The field report is being prepared.\";\n    this.reportTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n      this.reportTimer = null;\n      this.openReport();\n    }, delay);\n  }\n\n  openReport() {\n    if (this.reportOpen) return;\n    this.reportOpen = true;\n    this.dom.report.classList.add(\"is-open\");\n    this.dom.report.setAttribute(\"aria-hidden\", \"false\");\n    this.dom.specimen.inert = true;\n    this.dom.apparatus.inert = true;\n    this.dom.report.scrollTop = 0;\n    this.buildReport();\n    this.dom.report.focus({ preventScroll: true });\n    this.updateLiveUI(performance.now());\n    this.dom.announcer.textContent = \"Field report opened. \" + this.dom.outcomeLede.textContent;\n  }\n\n  closeReport(returnFocus) {\n    if (!this.reportOpen) return;\n    this.reportOpen = false;\n    this.dom.report.classList.remove(\"is-open\");\n    this.dom.report.setAttribute(\"aria-hidden\", \"true\");\n    this.dom.specimen.inert = false;\n    this.dom.apparatus.inert = false;\n    if (returnFocus) this.dom.playBtn.focus({ preventScroll: true });\n  }\n\n  buildReport() {\n    const sim = this.sim;\n    const final = sim.history[sim.history.length - 1];\n    const counts = final.counts;\n    const survivors = [\"flora\", \"prey\", \"predator\"].filter((key) => counts[key] > 0);\n    const events = sim.events;\n    const generation = final.gen;\n    this.dom.reportSeed.textContent = sim.seed;\n    this.dom.reportGenerations.textContent = generation;\n    this.dom.reportMeta.textContent = \"Seed \" + sim.seed + \" · \" + generation + \" generations · \" + (events.length ? events.length + \" disturbances\" : \"no disturbances\");\n    this.dom.reportDeck.textContent = \"A complete account of Specimen XIV — \" + generation + \" generations from founding seed to final census, drawn from the instrument's own record.\";\n    this.dom.ledgerCount.textContent = padNumber(events.length, 2) + (events.length === 1 ? \" event\" : \" events\");\n\n    let verdict = \"\";\n    if (counts.prey > 0 && counts.predator > 0) {\n      verdict = \"The seal held. Three kingdoms persisted; \" + (events.length ? \"the chamber endured \" + events.length + \" disturbances\" : \"the chamber ran undisturbed\") + \". \";\n    } else if (counts.prey === 0 && counts.predator === 0) {\n      verdict = \"The animal kingdom is absent. Flora remains, and the glass has grown very still. \";\n    } else if (counts.prey === 0) {\n      verdict = \"The grazers were lost. Hunters remain in a narrowing chamber, and the canopy closes over their paths. \";\n    } else if (counts.predator === 0) {\n      verdict = \"The hunters are gone. Grazers multiply against the glass, once more without a shadow above them. \";\n    } else {\n      verdict = \"The final census is incomplete; one kingdom persists without the other. \";\n    }\n    const driftInsight = this.describeDrift();\n    this.dom.outcomeLede.innerHTML = this.highlightCounts(verdict + (driftInsight || \"\"));\n\n    const rows = [\n      { key: \"flora\", label: \"Flora\", count: counts.flora, glyph: \"flora\", suffix: \"colonies\" },\n      { key: \"prey\", label: \"Grazers\", count: counts.prey, glyph: \"prey\", suffix: \"individuals\" },\n      { key: \"predator\", label: \"Hunters\", count: counts.predator, glyph: \"predator\", suffix: \"individuals\" }\n    ];\n    this.dom.finalCounts.innerHTML = rows.map((row) => {\n      const color = SPECIES[row.key].chartColor;\n      return '<div class=\"final-count\" style=\"--c:' + color + '\">'\n        + '<svg class=\"glyph\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><use href=\"#glyph-' + row.glyph + '\"/></svg>'\n        + '<span>' + row.label + '</span>'\n        + '<b>' + formatInteger(row.count) + '<small>' + row.suffix + '</small></b>'\n        + '</div>';\n    }).join(\"\");\n\n    if (events.length) {\n      this.dom.eventLedger.innerHTML = events.map((event) => {\n        const color = EVENT_META[event.type].chartColor;\n        return '<div class=\"event-row\" style=\"--event-c:' + color + '\">'\n          + '<svg class=\"glyph\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><use href=\"' + event.glyph + '\"/></svg>'\n          + '<span>' + event.label + '</span>'\n          + '<b>GEN ' + padNumber(event.gen, 3) + '</b>'\n          + '</div>';\n      }).join(\"\");\n    } else {\n      this.dom.eventLedger.innerHTML = '<div class=\"event-row\"><span>No disturbance was permitted.</span><b>—</b></div>';\n    }\n\n    this.dom.announcer.textContent = \"Observation complete after \" + generation + \" generations. \" + counts.flora + \" flora colonies, \" + counts.prey + \" grazers, \" + counts.predator + \" hunters. \" + events.length + \" disturbances.\";\n    requestAnimationFrame(() => this.reportRenderer.render());\n  }\n\n  highlightCounts(text) {\n    return text.replace(/(\\d[\\d,]*)/g, \"<b>$1</b>\");\n  }\n\n  describeDrift() {\n    const sim = this.sim;\n    const first = sim.history[0];\n    const last = sim.history[sim.history.length - 1];\n    let best = null;\n    for (const kind of [\"prey\", \"predator\"]) {\n      if (!first.genes[kind] || !last.genes[kind]) continue;\n      for (const gene of GENE_DEFS) {\n        const before = first.genes[kind][gene.key].mean;\n        const after = last.genes[kind][gene.key].mean;\n        const relative = Math.abs(after - before) / Math.max(0.001, gene.key === \"repro\" ? 60 : gene.key === \"vision\" ? 10 : 1.5);\n        if (!best || relative > best.relative) {\n          best = { kind, gene, before, after, relative };\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    if (!best || best.relative < 0.015) {\n      return \"The founding lineages held their form almost exactly.\";\n    }\n    const names = { prey: \"Grazers\", predator: \"Hunters\" };\n    const direction = best.after > best.before ? \"rose\" : \"contracted\";\n    return names[best.kind] + \"'\" + (best.kind === \"prey\" ? \"\" : \"\") + \" \" + best.gene.label.toLowerCase() + \" \" + direction\n      + \" from \" + formatGeneValue(best.gene.key, best.before) + \" to \" + formatGeneValue(best.gene.key, best.after)\n      + \" \" + best.gene.unit + \".\";\n  }\n\n  hideEventVisuals() {\n    this.dom.eventBanner.hidden = true;\n    this.dom.eventBanner.classList.remove(\"is-showing\", \"is-leaving\");\n    this.dom.eventWash.classList.remove(\"is-active\");\n    clearTimeout(this.bannerTimer);\n    clearTimeout(this.bannerHideTimer);\n    clearTimeout(this.washTimer);\n  }\n\n  updatePlaybackUI() {\n    const active = this.playing && !this.completed;\n    const complete = this.completed;\n    this.dom.playIcon.setAttribute(\"href\", active ? \"#icon-pause\" : \"#icon-play\");\n    this.dom.playBtn.setAttribute(\"aria-label\", active ? \"Pause observation\" : \"Resume observation\");\n    this.dom.playBtn.setAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", String(active));\n    this.dom.transportState.textContent = complete ? \"Complete\" : active ? \"Observing\" : \"Held\";\n    this.dom.worldWrap.classList.toggle(\"is-paused\", !active);\n    this.dom.liveTag.textContent = complete ? \"Observation sealed\" : active ? \"Live observation\" : \"Observation held\";\n    this.dom.metaState.textContent = complete ? \"Complete\" : active ? \"In progress\" : \"Held\";\n    this.dom.metaState.dataset.state = complete ? \"complete\" : active ? \"live\" : \"held\";\n    this.dom.sealWord.textContent = complete ? \"recorded\" : \"sealed\";\n  }\n\n  updateControlReadouts() {\n    this.dom.seedInput.value = String(this.state.seed);\n    this.dom.genInput.value = String(this.state.generations);\n    this.dom.mutationOutput.innerHTML = this.state.mutation.toFixed(1) + \"<small>% / gene</small>\";\n    this.dom.regrowthOutput.innerHTML = this.state.regrowth.toFixed(2) + \"<small>× baseline</small>\";\n    this.dom.speedOutput.innerHTML = Math.round(this.state.speed) + \"<small>gen / sec</small>\";\n    this.dom.intervalOutput.innerHTML = Math.round(this.state.catastropheEvery) + \"<small>generations</small>\";\n    this.dom.regrowthInput.value = String(this.state.regrowth);\n    this.dom.speedInput.value = String(this.state.speed);\n    this.dials.mutation.set(this.state.mutation);\n    this.dials.interval.set(this.state.catastropheEvery);\n    this.dom.regrowthInput.style.setProperty(\"--fill\", ((this.state.regrowth - 0.2) / 1.8 * 100).toFixed(2) + \"%\");\n    this.dom.speedInput.style.setProperty(\"--fill\", ((this.state.speed - 1) / 59 * 100).toFixed(2) + \"%\");\n    this.dom.regrowthInput.setAttribute(\"aria-valuetext\", this.state.regrowth.toFixed(2) + \" times baseline\");\n    this.dom.speedInput.setAttribute(\"aria-valuetext\", Math.round(this.state.speed) + \" generations per second\");\n    this.dom.regrowthInput.value = String(this.state.regrowth);\n    this.updateToggles();\n  }\n\n  updateToggles() {\n    this.dom.toggles.forEach((toggle) => {\n      const on = !!this.state.events[toggle.dataset.event];\n      toggle.setAttribute(\"aria-checked\", String(on));\n    });\n    const count = this.enabledEventTypes().length;\n    this.dom.disturbanceCount.textContent = count ? count + (count === 1 ? \" active\" : \" active\") : \"muted\";\n    this.dom.eventLedger && null;\n  }\n\n  syncControls() {\n    this.updateControlReadouts();\n    this.dom.metaSeed.textContent = String(this.state.seed);\n  }\n\n  updateAll(includeFieldNote) {\n    this.updateControlReadouts();\n    this.updatePlaybackUI();\n    this.updateLiveUI(performance.now());\n    this.dom.metaSeed.textContent = String(this.state.seed);\n    if (includeFieldNote) this.updateFieldNote(true);\n  }\n\n  updateLiveUI(now) {\n    if (!this.dom) return;\n    const counts = this.sim.currentCounts();\n    this.dom.countFlora.textContent = formatInteger(counts.flora);\n    this.dom.countPrey.textContent = formatInteger(counts.prey);\n    this.dom.countPredator.textContent = formatInteger(counts.predator);\n    this.dom.hudGen.textContent = padNumber(this.sim.gen, 3);\n    this.dom.genNow.textContent = padNumber(this.sim.gen, 3);\n    this.dom.hudTarget.textContent = String(this.state.generations);\n    this.dom.genTarget.textContent = String(this.state.generations);\n    this.dom.metaSeed.textContent = String(this.state.seed);\n    const progress = this.state.generations ? this.sim.gen / this.state.generations : 0;\n    const circumference = 273.32;\n    this.dom.sealProgress.style.strokeDashoffset = String(circumference * (1 - clamp(progress, 0, 1)));\n    this.dom.noteSerial.textContent = \"XIV / \" + padNumber(this.sim.gen, 3);\n    this.dom.eventLedger && null;\n    this.dom.worldCanvas.setAttribute(\"aria-label\",\n      \"A living terrarium at generation \" + this.sim.gen + \" of \" + this.state.generations\n      + \", containing \" + counts.flora + \" flora colonies, \" + counts.prey + \" grazers and \" + counts.predator + \" hunters.\");\n    if (now - this.lastNote > 1400) {\n      this.lastNote = now;\n      this.updateFieldNote(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  updateFieldNote(force) {\n    if (!this.sim) return;\n    const now = performance.now();\n    if (!force && now - this.lastNote < 1400) return;\n    const h = this.sim.history;\n    const current = h[h.length - 1];\n    const previous = h[Math.max(0, h.length - 16)];\n    const preyDelta = current.counts.prey - previous.counts.prey;\n    const predatorDelta = current.counts.predator - previous.counts.predator;\n    const active = this.sim.effectState();\n    let note;\n    if (active.drought) {\n      note = \"The soil draws back from the glass. Growth has stopped; every grazer is ranging.\";\n    } else if (active.plague) {\n      note = \"A pale dust moves through the herd. The next census will be smaller than the last.\";\n    } else if (active.bloom) {\n      note = \"The water has gone green and bright. The grazers feed upon their own undoing.\";\n    } else if (current.counts.prey === 0 && current.counts.predator === 0) {\n      note = \"The hunters and grazers are gone. Only the canopy remains, quietly closing over the paths.\";\n    } else if (current.counts.prey === 0) {\n      note = \"The grazers are gone. Hunters circle a silent canopy, and the glass feels very large.\";\n    } else if (current.counts.predator === 0) {\n      note = \"No hunters remain. The grazers multiply against the glass, without a shadow in the grass.\";\n    } else if (predatorDelta > 4) {\n      note = \"Hunter sign is rising at the eastern edge. The herd has begun to move as one.\";\n    } else if (preyDelta > 8) {\n      note = \"The grazers are increasing. New paths are being worn through the canopy.\";\n    } else if (preyDelta < -8) {\n      note = \"Grazer numbers are falling. The canopy closes quietly behind them.\";\n    } else if (preyDelta > 0) {\n      note = \"The chamber is near balance. Grazers press inward; the hunters keep a patient distance.\";\n    } else if (this.sim.gen < 8) {\n      note = \"The founding colony is settling into its glass. The first census is being taken.\";\n    } else {\n      note = \"A watchful quiet. The canopy breathes; the grazing lines are moving slowly.\";\n    }\n    if (this.dom.fieldNote.textContent !== note) this.dom.fieldNote.textContent = note;\n  }\n\n  drawTimeline(now) {\n    const { ctx, width, height } = fitCanvas(this.dom.timelineCanvas);\n    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n    if (width < 10 || height < 10) return;\n    const pad = { left: 3, right: 3, top: 16, bottom: 8 };\n    const plotW = width - pad.left - pad.right;\n    const target = Math.max(1, this.state.generations);\n    const progress = clamp(this.sim.gen / target, 0, 1);\n    const trackY = Math.round(pad.top + (height - pad.top - pad.bottom) * 0.58) + 0.5;\n    const eventColor = (type) => ({ drought: \"#e48b57\", plague: \"#dc7d99\", bloom: \"#75d4c7\" }[type] || \"#c8ad74\");\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(222, 216, 195, .25)\";\n    ctx.lineWidth = 0.65;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(pad.left, trackY);\n    ctx.lineTo(pad.left + plotW, trackY);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    const tickX = (fraction) => pad.left + fraction * plotW;\n    ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(222, 216, 195, .2)\";\n    for (let i = 0; i <= 8; i++) {\n      const x = tickX(i / 8);\n      const tall = i % 2 === 0;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(x, trackY - (tall ? 4 : 2));\n      ctx.lineTo(x, trackY + (tall ? 4 : 2));\n      ctx.stroke();\n    }\n    ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(238, 232, 216, .75)\";\n    ctx.lineWidth = 0.85;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(pad.left, trackY);\n    ctx.lineTo(pad.left + progress * plotW, trackY);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"#9b7a40\";\n    for (const event of this.sim.events) {\n      const x = tickX(clamp(event.gen / target, 0, 1));\n      const color = eventColor(event.type);\n      ctx.fillStyle = color;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(x, trackY - 7);\n      ctx.lineTo(x + 3.3, trackY - 3.7);\n      ctx.lineTo(x, trackY - 0.5);\n      ctx.lineTo(x - 3.3, trackY - 3.7);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.fill();\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55;\n      ctx.font = '400 5.2px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n      ctx.textAlign = \"center\";\n      ctx.fillStyle = color;\n      ctx.fillText(event.roman, x, trackY - 10);\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n    }\n    const nextAt = this.sim.nextDisturbanceAt();\n    if (!this.completed && nextAt <= target) {\n      const x = tickX(clamp(nextAt / target, 0, 1));\n      ctx.strokeStyle = \"rgba(238, 232, 216, .3)\";\n      ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;\n      ctx.setLineDash([1, 2]);\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(x, trackY - 4);\n      ctx.lineTo(x, trackY + 4);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.setLineDash([]);\n    }\n    const headX = tickX(progress);\n    ctx.fillStyle = this.completed ? \"#c8ad74\" : \"#eee8d8\";\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(headX, trackY, this.playing ? 2.4 : 2, 0, TAU);\n    ctx.fill();\n    if (this.playing) {\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3;\n      ctx.strokeStyle = \"#eee8d8\";\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(headX, trackY, 4.6 + 1.6 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(now * 0.003)), 0, TAU);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n    }\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(222, 216, 195, .4)\";\n    ctx.font = '400 5.5px \"JetBrains Mono\", monospace';\n    ctx.textAlign = \"left\";\n    ctx.textBaseline = \"top\";\n    ctx.fillText(\"000\", pad.left, trackY + 9);\n    ctx.textAlign = \"center\";\n    ctx.fillText(\"GENERATION \" + padNumber(this.sim.gen, 3) + \" / \" + String(target).padStart(3, \"0\"), pad.left + plotW * 0.5, trackY + 9);\n    ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n    ctx.fillText(String(target).padStart(3, \"0\"), pad.left + plotW, trackY + 9);\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  drawMicros(now, force = false) {\n    if (!force && now - this.lastMicro < 220) return;\n    for (const [type, canvas] of this.dom.micros) {\n      if (!canvas || !canvas.isConnected) continue;\n      const { ctx, width, height } = fitCanvas(canvas);\n      if (width < 8 || height < 8) continue;\n      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n      let series = [];\n      let color = \"#c8ad74\";\n      if (type === \"seed\") {\n        series = seededAmplitude(this.state.seed);\n        color = \"#c8ad74\";\n      } else if (type === \"mutation\") {\n        series = this.sim.history.slice(-48).map((h) => h.births ? h.mutations / h.births : 0);\n        color = \"#d8c18e\";\n      } else if (type === \"regrowth\") {\n        series = this.sim.history.slice(-50).map((h) => h.counts.flora);\n        color = \"#b8dd7c\";\n      } else if (type === \"speed\") {\n        series = this.throughput.slice(-36);\n        color = \"#c8ad74\";\n      } else if (type === \"generations\") {\n        series = this.sim.history.slice(-50).map((h) => h.counts.prey + h.counts.predator);\n        color = \"#b8dd7c\";\n      } else if (type === \"interval\") {\n        series = this.sim.history.slice(-60).map((h) => h.counts.prey + h.counts.predator);\n        color = \"#e48b57\";\n      }\n      if (!series.length) series = [0, 0];\n      const min = Math.min(...series);\n      const max = Math.max(...series);\n      const flat = max - min < 0.00001;\n      const padY = 3;\n      const xAt = (index) => 1 + (index / Math.max(1, series.length - 1)) * (width - 2);\n      const yAt = (value) => flat ?
